Hi, doctor. Thank you so much for seeing me. I really hope I'm not taking up too much of your time. 

Um... I guess I came in today because... well, I've been having this little issue. It's probably just stress, I don't want you to think I'm crazy or anything like that. But... lately, even when my house is completely quiet and I know for a fact nobody else is there, I keep hearing... well, I hear voices. 

It sounds as clear as somebody standing right there in my little studio with me. It says my name, and... it tells me that my work is being judged. Which is upsetting, because I really do try my best at my job, I promise I do. I'm a good worker. I just... I wanted to see if maybe there's a simple explanation for it? Am I explaining that okay for you?

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Oh... um. Well, seeing or feeling things, no, definitely not. I promise I don't see things that aren't there. I know that sounds very... well, very concerning, and I wouldn't want you to think I was imagining things like that. 

But... well, I suppose... I mean, the reason I actually came in to see you... I hope this doesn't sound too strange, doctor. I really try to keep things quiet and not bother anyone. But lately, when I'm just sitting in my little studio area with my paintings, and the house is completely quiet... I've been hearing things. 

Just... voices. They sound so clear, exactly like someone is standing right there in the room with me. They say my name, and... and they tell me my work is being judged. Even when I know for a fact nobody else is home. 

I know it sounds awful, and I'm so embarrassed to even admit it out loud. I don't want you to think I'm losing my mind or anything. It's just a little stress, probably, right? Do you think that's normal when someone is under a lot of pressure? I really hope I'm not making too much of a fuss over it.

Oh, um... well... I don't know if this is exactly what you mean. I really don't want to sound arrogant or anything, because I know I'm just a personal care aide and I haven't even finished high school, but...

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Oh, um... no, doctor. No, I don't think so. I mean, besides what I already told you about the agency and my paintings... there really hasn't been anything else.

People restart sentences, pause\n  to think, trail off, and come at the point sideways — let yours do that where it would.\n- Let [Conversation Style] shape how much you say, how you word it, and how readily you\n  disclose it.\n- Speak in the first person, and let how you feel about what you are saying come through.\n- If you are handed the same item again, say a different part of it, or answer as someone who\n  knows they have already said this — not the same sentence twice.\n- If [Targeted Info] is \"None\", answer as a person who simply does not have that to give: say\n  you are not sure, say you have not noticed it, ask what the doctor means, or say what you do\n  know and stop there. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Oh, um... like a movie? No, no, nothing like aliens or... or magic or anything like that. I really wouldn't want you to think I was completely losing my grip on reality. 

I mean, the only thing... and I know I already mentioned this, and I feel so silly bringing it up again because I know how it sounds... but the only thing was that business with the other home-care agency and the people from the library. 

I just... I was so certain they were watching me. Watching my paintings, looking at my client papers. I thought they were trying to steal this... this big chance that was meant for me. And I know people tried to tell me it didn't make any sense, that they wouldn't do that. But at the time, I just *knew* they were. It felt so real to me. 

But it wasn't like a science fiction movie, I promise. Just... a misunderstanding, I suppose. I really hope I'm not sounding too crazy by repeating it. I'm just trying to answer your question the best I can... is that what you meant?

Oh, Dr... I... I really don't know what else to say, and I feel just awful that I can't give you a new answer. I promise I'm trying my very best to be helpful, I really am. I don't want you to be disappointed in me.

It's just... well, it always comes back to that same exact thing. And looking back now, I do see how... well, how strange it sounds. Even when everyone told me it made no sense, I just couldn't let it go. I really believed it back then. 

But I promise you, I don't go around believing impossible things all the time. That was just a... a bad patch, I suppose. I'm so sorry I don't have a different example for you. I hope you're not too frustrated with me... am I doing alright answering your questions?
